Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in galactic theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 709 and the invariant I(A,B) = 790 mod 2.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to galactic theory, we decompose the problem into 709 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 790. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.
